**What + How:** the R7/R13 decomposition rests on two invariants stated in `mios_dispatch.py`'s header but enforced nowhere: (1) each extracted `mios_pipe/**` module is a **one-way boundary** — it must NOT `import server` / `from server import …`; (2) `server.py` must **re-import verbatim** every public symbol it moved out, preserving the original alias (surface parity). Add a new `check_pipe_extraction_parity()` to `automation/38-drift-checks.sh` (next free check number) that: greps every `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/mios_pipe/**/*.py` for a forbidden `^\s*(from|import)\s+server\b` and FAILS listing offenders; and, using a tiny helper (a Python AST walk invoked from the check, e.g. `tools/pipe-parity-check.py`), asserts that for each `mios_pipe` module exposing names re-imported by `server.py`, the `from mios_pipe.… import (…)` line in `server.py` lists them (no silent drop). Follow the existing `check_*` function style + wire into `main`; keep it read-only + fast (no running server).
**Where:** `automation/38-drift-checks.sh`; new `tools/pipe-parity-check.py`; a negative case in `tests/drift-gate-negatives.sh` (inject `import server` into a temp module copy → assert red → restore → green).
**Done When:** `bash -n automation/38-drift-checks.sh tests/drift-gate-negatives.sh` clean; `python3 -m py_compile tools/pipe-parity-check.py` passes; the gate is green on HEAD, reds when a `mios_pipe` module adds `import server`, and reds when a re-import line drops a moved symbol; `just drift-gate` green. Stage ONLY explicit paths.

## AGY-351  (WS-PIPE, **P1 security**) — Audit + per-site justify/guard the 10 verb backends still containing `eval`  **[DONE]**
**Who:** you (bash; security follow-up to AGY-41/AGY-67). **When:** independent; security priority.
**What + How:** AGY-41 removed the agent-arg `eval` surface, but `grep -rl "eval " usr/libexec/mios/` still returns 10 files: `mios-ai-clear`, `mios-ai-reset`, `mios-cron-director`, `mios-daemon`, `mios-day0-reset`, `mios-directory-lookup`, `mios-launch`, `mios-rag`, `mios-tool-search`, `mios-window`. For EACH remaining `eval`, verify provenance and either (a) rewrite to a safe form (quoted `"$@"` arrays / `case`-allowlist dispatch / `printf %q`) when any input is agent-derived, or (b) if load-bearing with NO agent-controlled input, annotate the exact line with a standardized `# TD-1: eval-safe, input=<source>, not agent-controlled` comment (per the flatten "don't blind-delete load-bearing" caution). Confirmed live sites to resolve include `usr/libexec/mios/mios-launch:507` (`eval exec "$_hit_cmd" '"$@"'`) and `usr/libexec/mios/mios-window:386` (`eval "$(python3 - "$sub" "$hwnd" …)`) — trace each argument back to a non-agent source or convert it. Do NOT touch the medicat/theme trees; stage only the verb files you edit.
**Where:** the 10 files under `usr/libexec/mios/` listed above (only those you actually change/annotate).
**Done When:** every remaining `eval` in `usr/libexec/mios/` is EITHER converted to a non-re-parsing form OR carries the `# TD-1: eval-safe, input=…` justification; `grep -rn "TD-1: eval-safe" usr/libexec/mios/` accounts for each surviving `eval`; `bash -n` clean on every touched file; the AGY-41 eval-injection drift-check (in `automation/38-drift-checks.sh`) stays green; `just drift-gate` green. Stage ONLY the explicit verb paths you edited.

## AGY-352  (WS-PIPE, **P1 security**) — Harden the SSOT template renderer + dispatch builder against placeholder/arg-splice injection  **[DONE]**
**Who:** you (Python; security). **When:** after AGY-351 (same injection theme), independent code path.
**What + How:** `mios_pipe/../mios_template.py::_template_to_cmd` (the sole verb command-template renderer, `_TEMPLATE_PH_RE = \{([a-zA-Z_]\w*)(?:(=|\?|!|\*)([^}]*))?\}`) already `shlex.quote`s every substituted value, and `mios_dispatch.py::_build_dispatch_cmd` routes verbs through it. Add defense-in-depth: (1) a **placeholder-name allowlist** check — a rendered placeholder name that resolves via `_arg_with_synonyms` to nothing AND is not a declared arg for the verb should render empty, never leak a literal `{name}` into the bash line; add an assertion/log for unknown placeholder names. (2) A regression test proving no agent-supplied value can escape quoting: feed adversarial args (`$(id)`, `;rm -rf /`, backticks, `foo bar`, newline) through `_template_to_cmd` for a representative `[verbs.*].cmd` and assert the dangerous metacharacters appear only inside a single `shlex.quote`d token. (3) Confirm the `{arg=$ENVVAR:fallback}` ENV-default branch (L96-99) cannot read an attacker-chosen env name (the `ENVVAR` comes from the template author, not args) — add a test asserting args cannot influence which env var is read.
**Where:** `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/mios_template.py` (guard + log only — do not change quoting semantics); extend `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/test_mios_template.py` with the adversarial cases; if a builder-level guard is warranted, `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/mios_dispatch.py::_build_dispatch_cmd`.
**Done When:** `python3 -m py_compile mios_template.py mios_dispatch.py` passes; the new adversarial tests in `test_mios_template.py` prove every metacharacter stays quoted and unknown placeholders render empty (not literal); no existing `test_mios_template.py` case regresses; `just drift-gate` green. Stage ONLY explicit paths.

But `root-manifest.json` records the LONG form as `proposed_canonical` (`MIOS_AI_VLLM_SERVED_NAME`, `MIOS_AI_VLLM_GPU_UTIL`, …, `MIOS_AI_SGLANG_*` — ~13 entries, L1022-1099). Make the short form authoritative: update those `proposed_canonical` values to the short `MIOS_VLLM_*`/`MIOS_SGLANG_*` names (matching `_alias_for` in the generator), and add a drift-check `check_vllm_name_canonical()` to `automation/38-drift-checks.sh` that FAILS if any `MIOS_AI_VLLM_*`/`MIOS_AI_SGLANG_*` string appears as an *emitted or proposed-canonical* name anywhere under the tree (allowlisting only historical/manifest audit fields if genuinely needed). Keep `userenv.sh` and `tools/lib/userenv.sh` twins byte-identical (drift-27).
**Where:** `root-manifest.json` (the ~13 `proposed_canonical` lines only); `automation/38-drift-checks.sh` (new check + wire into `main`); a negative case in `tests/drift-gate-negatives.sh`; verify (do not diverge) `tools/generate-names-registry.py` + `usr/lib/mios/userenv.sh`.
**Done When:** `grep -rn "MIOS_AI_VLLM_\|MIOS_AI_SGLANG_" root-manifest.json` returns nothing (all reconciled to short); the new gate is green on HEAD and reds if a `MIOS_AI_VLLM_*` canonical is reintroduced; `bash -n automation/38-drift-checks.sh` clean; userenv twins still identical; `just drift-gate` green. Stage ONLY explicit paths.

## AGY-354  (WS-PIPE, **P2**) — Compiled-template system: precompile every verb `cmd` at catalog load, drop per-dispatch regex  **[DONE]**
**Who:** you (Python). **When:** builds on AGY-352 (same renderer); independent of the extractions.
**What + How:** today `mios_template.py::_template_to_cmd` re-runs `_TEMPLATE_PH_RE.sub` (a fresh regex parse) on **every** dispatch, and the placeholder-name/arg linkage is validated nowhere. Add a compile step: a new `mios_template.compile_template(template) -> CompiledTemplate` that parses the placeholders ONCE into an ordered list of `(literal | Placeholder(name, op, rest))` segments and returns a callable/dataclass; `_template_to_cmd` becomes a thin `compile_template(template).render(tool, args)` with an LRU/dict cache keyed by the template string (templates are static SSOT text, so cache is safe and bounded by verb count). Precompile all `[verbs.*].cmd` / `cmd_args` / `cmd_positioned` / `cmd_pixel` / `cmd_resize` variants at catalog-load time (where `mios_dispatch.configure(verb_catalog=…)` receives the catalog) and surface a `CompiledTemplate.placeholder_names` set for the AGY-355 validation gate. Preserve all placeholder semantics (`{arg}`, `{arg!}`, `{arg=default}` incl. `$ENV:fallback`, `{arg?FLAG}`, `{arg*}`) and the None-on-abort behavior byte-for-byte.
**Where:** `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/mios_template.py` (add `compile_template`/`CompiledTemplate`, keep `_template_to_cmd` as the compat entry); `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/mios_dispatch.py` (precompile at `configure()` time); extend `usr/lib/mios/agent-pipe/test_mios_template.py`.
**Done When:** `python3 -m py_compile mios_template.py mios_dispatch.py` passes; every existing `test_mios_template.py` case passes unchanged (semantic parity); a new test asserts `compile_template(t).render(tool,args) == <old _template_to_cmd output>` for each placeholder form and that the compiled cache returns the SAME object for a repeated template; `just drift-gate` green. Stage ONLY explicit paths.

**Who:** you (bash + GitHub Actions). **What+How:** `just drift-gate` (Justfile lines 99-102) runs BOTH `automation/38-drift-checks.sh` AND `tests/drift-gate-negatives.sh` (the 16-case inject-assert-restore harness whose `main()` calls test_version_ssot…test_containerfile_pinned_clones), but the CI `drift-gate` job in `.github/workflows/mios-ci.yml` runs only `lint-shell.sh`, `38-ssot-lint.sh`, the agent-pipe tests, docgen, and `38-drift-checks.sh` — the negative harness NEVER runs in CI, so a refactor that silently neuters a gate (makes it stop biting) passes CI. ## AGY-368  (WS-TESTDOC, **P1**) — ADR-0012: float-latest / no-hand-pinned-version principle (generalize ADR-0003 across every artifact class)  **[DONE]**
**Who:** you (Markdown ADR + docs). **What+How:** ADR-0003 (usr/share/doc/mios/adr/0003-sbom-not-hardcode.md) decided container-image digests are build-resolved SBOM data, and its Status explicitly leaves "model checksums, package version-hashes … as PLANNED follow-up" — but the OPERATOR PRINCIPLE "NO hand-pinned versions anywhere — everything floats latest, resolved at build + recorded in SBOM" is not itself an accepted decision, and its live enforcement is scattered (check_containerfile_pinned_clones "(71)" wants git clones pinned to a REF; WS-MIOSSYS/WS-RELTOP "newest-packages-globally tagged-at-build"; the firstboot-tier that evicts the vLLM/SGLang whales carrying `:latest` intent). ## AGY-370  (WS-TESTDOC, **P1**) — ADR-0014: the bootc-install bare-metal leg (bootc install to-disk --transport oci)  **[DONE]**
**Who:** you (Markdown ADR + docs). **What+How:** The bare-metal install leg is the least-done deploy path and is undocumented as a decision: automation/build-mios.sh (line ~517) only offers `bootc install to-existing-root --source-imgref localhost/mios:latest` (converts an ALREADY-running host in place), and installation/mios-install.sh's `bootc` target (resolve_bootc, line 228) does only `switch` (no-op unless the host is already bootc-booted) or `upgrade` (→ mios-update) — there is NO fresh-disk `bootc install to-disk` and NO offline `--transport oci` path to install the pinned image onto blank hardware (the gap the FOSS-upstream memo names). Note it as `status: proposed` (implementation PLANNED, not done). Frontmatter: `laws: [3, 4, 12]`, real `ssot_keys` (image.sidecars, build.bake, and the ventoy/kickstart keys check_deploy_plane reads), `related_ws: [WS-CAT, WS-MDRIVE]`, cross-links to ADR-0005 + ADR-0008 in References. **Done When:** gate flags an injected hard pin; green on the floated tree.

## WS-GHSELF — GitHub self-sufficiency (fit the stock runner)

## AGY-403  (WS-GHSELF, **P1**) — split build.sh into resumable RUN layers  **[DONE]**
**What:** the monolithic `STEP 17/25` (build.sh, ~65 stages) is ONE layer — a mid-bake reboot restarts the whole ~1-2h. Split the Containerfile so build.sh runs in a few checkpointed RUN layers (e.g. overlay+packages | services+render | bake+finalize) so podman's layer cache resumes near the failure. This is what makes the local publish reboot-cheap AND helps GitHub.
**Where:** `Containerfile`, `automation/build.sh`.
**Done When:** a reboot mid-build resumes from the last completed RUN layer, not stage 1.

## AGY-404  (WS-GHSELF, P2) — firstboot-tier eviction expansion  **[DONE]**

**Where:** `plan.d/firstboot.list`, `mios.toml [build.bake].firstboot_tokens`, `mios-ai-firstboot`/`mios-bound-images-firstboot`.
**Done When:** firstboot-tier invariant (check-72) green; core stays offline.

## AGY-405  (WS-GHSELF, P2) — GitHub PUBLISH gate flips true when store fits  **[DONE]**
**What:** once AGY-394..404 shrink the store under the runner, flip the `PUBLISH` capacity gate so GitHub bakes+pushes too (GitHub≡Forgejo equal publishers). Add a preflight that measures free /mnt vs projected store and skips gracefully if still too big.
**Where:** `.github/workflows/mios-ci.yml`.
**Done When:** GitHub bakes+pushes when it fits; skips cleanly when it doesn't.

## AGY-406  (WS-GHSELF, P3) — Forgejo publisher parity check  **[DONE]**

# BATCH 2 (2026-07-31) — AGY-479..730: Global Unification Plan (GUP) + program decomposition

> Ref: `usr/share/doc/mios/reference/GLOBAL-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md`. INVARIANT for every GUP task:
> after your change, `mios-env-snapshot | diff env-baseline.txt -` must be EMPTY (or show ONLY the
> exact keys your task intends to drop). Non-empty diff = revert. Stage explicit paths; `agy:` prefix.

## GUP Phase 0 — freeze, baseline, lossless gate (do these FIRST, in order)

**What:** `usr/libexec/mios/mios-env-snapshot` (bash, AI-hint, set -e) that sources the resolver in a clean env and prints `env | grep ^MIOS_ | sort`. The ground-truth capture for every lossless diff. **Where:** new libexec. **Done:** template-conformance PASS; output is deterministic across runs.

## AGY-480  (WS-UNIFY, **P0**) — check_resolved_env_lossless drift-gate  **[DONE]**
**What:** commit `usr/share/mios/reference/env-baseline.txt` (mios-env-snapshot output) + a check that re-snapshots and diffs vs the committed baseline; FAIL on any drift not accompanied by a deliberate baseline bump (a `MIOS_ENV_BASELINE_BUMP` marker in the commit). Register in main() + gate-index + a negative test. **Where:** `98-drift-checks.sh`, `drift-gate-index.tsv`, `tests/drift-gate-negatives.sh`, baseline file. **Done:** gate green on current tree; flags an injected value change.

## AGY-481  (WS-UNIFY, **P0**) — commit AGY vendoring/tools to a clean base  **[DONE]**
**What:** commit the verified uncommitted work: vendored k3s v1.36.2 (binary+sha+install+selinux), `mios-resolve-latest`, `mios-vendor-refresh`, `mios-web`/`mios-data` Containerfiles, check-87 + its negative, terra.key + terra.repo file:// gpgkey, VERSIONS.txt, MiOS-SBOM.csv/Justfile/names.generated/OFFLINE-FIRST updates. Verify each passes drift-gate; explicit paths only. **Where:** the listed files. **Done:** tree clean; `just drift-gate` green.
